<div className="rev-diff">
<div className="hunk">
<p className="lineno">{"Line 248"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"When Fatigue penalizes both Accuracy and defensive rolls, Weapon Parry suffers only the defensive roll penalty. If something penalizes only Accuracy, Weapon Parry suffers that penalty. "}</p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"#### Escape Grapple (Speed 6 + X, Delay X, Special, Interrupt)"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"Escape Grapple may only be used by the defender in a grapple. When you attempt to escape, you make a grapple check against everyone you are currently grappled with. The \"X\" in the technique's Speed and Delay is the amount you fail your grapple check by, or the highest amount you fail by, if you are in multiple grapples. The minimum speed of the escape grapple is speed 4."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- If you roll a 12 against your opponent's 16, you fail by 4. Escape Grapple has Speed 8 and Delay 8 (4 + 4)."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- If you roll a 24 against your opponent's 16, you succeed by 8. Escape Grapple has Speed 4 and Delay 4."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- If you roll a 30 against your opponent's 16, you succeed by 14. Escape Grapple costs Speed 4 and Delay 4 (minimum)."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"A successful Escape Grapple ends all grapples you are currently in. The technique has a Delay equal to its final Speed, and cannot be Aborted despite its Delay."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"**Important Interactions:**"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- This action does not require free hands and can be performed even when your hands are restrained or occupied."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- If you are in multiple grapples, you must roll against each grappler's grapple check. The highest amount you fail by determines X."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- Escape Grapple can be attempted even if you have already taken an action this IC, as it is a defensive response to being grappled."}</del></p>
<p className="ctx">{"#### Perform Handseals (Speed Variable, Abort)"}</p>
</div>

<p className="ctx">{"## Conditions and Status Effects"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"### [Wounded](/Combat/wounds)"}</p>

<p className="ctx">{"### Fatigued"}</p>
</div>
<div className="hunk">
<p className="lineno">{"Line 351"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"### Status Effects"}</p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"#### Airborne"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"Airborne represents being lifted into the air, violently or otherwise. An Airborne ninja ignores all Ground-tagged effects and ground-based environmental hazards, but similarly cannot benefit from effects that require contact with the ground, and cannot be knocked Prone. They may still defend themselves, but their move speed is reduced to 0, potentially impacting their ability to escape areas of effect. The status ends on the Airborne target's next non-interrupt action, unless stated otherwise."}</ins></p>
<p className="ctx">{"#### Bleed"}</p>
</div>
<div className="hunk">
<p className="lineno">{"Line 371"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"Buried Status applieds a immobilization penalty equal to twice its rating. You can dig yourself out of being buried equal to the immobilization penalty you are suffering in speed. Which means buried 4 would require a speed 8 action to dig yourself out."}</p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"#### Grounded"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"Grounded represents being submerged at least partially in the ground. A Grounded character's move speed is reduced to 0, and any penalties inflicted on them by the Prone condition are doubled, until they remove themselves from the ground as a Speed 10, Delay 5 action. Buried characters cannot be knocked Airborne, and applying the Buried condition removes the Airborne condition."}</ins></p>
<p className="ctx">{"#### Ignite"}</p>
</div>
<div className="hunk">
<p className="lineno">{"Line 391"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"#### Paralysis"}</p>
<p className="chg">{"Paralysis"}<del>{"differs from immobilization in that paralysis"}</del>{" is an internal"}<del>{"or mental"}</del>{" effect which impedes your ability to "}<del>{"move, whereas immobilization is generally used for effects"}</del><ins>{"properly use your own muscles, temporarily causing spasms and twitches that prevent you from going full force,"}</ins>{" which "}<del>{"somehow physically restrain you."}</del><ins>{"many ninja like to do, as it turns out."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg">{"When paralyzed, "}<ins>{"you treat"}</ins>{" the"}<del>{"base Speed of all your actions is increased by the paralysis rating, to a maximum of double their original Speed. <p className="chg">{"For example, at Paralysis 3 your "}<del>{"basic unarmed attack"}</del><ins>{"Speed 8 Basic Attack"}</ins>{" would "}<del>{"be"}</del><ins>{"deal damage as if it were a"}</ins>{" Speed "}<del>{"11;"}</del><ins>{"5 attack; if"}</ins>{" you "}<del>{"could reduce it"}</del><ins>{"spent AP"}</ins>{" to "}<del>{"a minimum of"}</del><ins>{"reduce its"}</ins>{" Speed "}<del>{"(11/2 = 5.5) 6 with AP, and then"}</del><ins>{"to 4,"}</ins>{" it would "}<del>{"multiply your"}</del><ins>{"then be treated as a Speed 2 attack for its"}</ins>{" damage "}<del>{"bonus"}</del><ins>{"(because it can’t reduce it"}</ins>{" by "}<del>{"(6 - 3) 3. That same Paralysis would change your dodge to a"}</del><ins>{"more than half, and its"}</ins>{" Speed "}<del>{"4 Interrupt (meaning that some extremely quick, previously-avoidable attacks might become undodgeable)."}</del><ins>{"is 4)."}</ins></p>
<p className="ctx">{"Paralysis effects do not combine. If you are afflicted with two paralysis effects at once, the more potent one overwrites the less severe one. If they both have the same severity, the one with the longer duration overwrites the other."}</p>

<div className="hunk">
<p className="lineno">{"Line 407"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"Make Resistance roll against the poison's Toxicology roll. Failure: take Severity damage per IC for the duration. Multiple poisons can exist simultaneously, but only the highest Poison and one set of Special Effects apply. Same source only renews duration."}</p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"#### Shock"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"Similar to Paralysis but more of a general impediment to one’s ability to move and act, Shock effects slow down ordinarily fast-moving, fast-acting shinobi, making it easier to outmaneuver an enemy before they can recover."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"Shock increases the effective Speed of all non-Variable Speed Actions by its Severity; a Shock 3 would increase a Basic Unarmed Attack from Speed 8 to Speed 11, but would deal damage based off of its original Speed (in this case, 8). If it then had its Speed reduced (such as with AP), it could then be reduced to a minimum of Speed (11/2 = 5.5) 6 with AP, and deal damage as a Speed 6 attack; in essence, AP spent counteracts this status effect until you begin to lower its Speed below that of its unshocked amount."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"Shock does apply to Interrupt actions, but at only half its severity, rounded down. Performing a Dodge (normally Speed 2) with a Shock 4 would increase its Speed to 4 (meaning that some extremely quick, previously-avoidable attacks might become undodgeable)."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"Shock effects do not combine, and no action can have its speed more than doubled regardless of your Shock severity. If you are afflicted with two Shock effects at once, the more potent one overwrites the less severe one. If they both have the same severity, the one with the longer duration overwrites the other."}</ins></p>
<p className="ctx">{"#### Sleep"}</p>
</div>

<p className="ctx">{"## **Recovery**"}</p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"Downtime can be spent in numerous ways, but chiefly, it’s your recovery resource."}</del><ins>{"Hitpoints,"}</ins>{" Vitality, "}<del>{"hitpoints, fatigue,"}</del><ins>{"Fatigue"}</ins>{" and "}<del>{"willpower all require Downtime to be spent in order to recover them. They can be spent"}</del><ins>{"Willpower are reset"}</ins>{" at the "}<del>{"following amounts to recover your character;"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- 1 Downtime to recover 25% of your max vitality"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- 1 Downtime to recover 20%"}</del><ins>{"start"}</ins>{" of "}<del>{"your max hitpoints"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- 1 Downtime to recover 1 Fatigue level"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- 4 Downtime to recover your WP up to your WP maximum"}</del><ins>{"every scene or event. Wounds heal on their own timer (see Wound Recovery on the Wounds page) and cannot be recovered with Downtime."}</ins></p>

<div className="rev-diff">
<div className="hunk">
<p className="lineno">{"Line 19"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"<br />"}</p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"## **Wound Recovery**"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"Wounds heal on a flat timer based on their severity. Downtime cannot be spent to recover from wounds."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"| Severity | Recovery |"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"| :-- | :-- |"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"| **Minor** | Heals at the end of the battle |"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"| **Major** | 4 days of healing |"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"| **Severe** | 8 days of healing |"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"| **Critical** | 16 days of healing. Only heals on days you roleplay in a Hospital or with a Doctor |"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"Healing is counted in real-world (OOC) days. Each day, every wound you have heals by:"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"| That day | Days of healing |"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"| :-- | :-- |"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"| No medical roleplay | 1 |"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"| Roleplay for at least an hour in a Hospital | 2 |"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"| Roleplay for at least an hour with a character who has the Doctor ability | 4 |"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"A Doctor can provide this to a number of patients at the same time equal to their ranks in the Doctor ability. This limit is doubled when they treat patients in a Hospital."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"---"}</ins></p>

<p className="ctx">{"What would exhaust most shinobi leaves you merely inconvenienced. Your chakra replenishes with a consistency that has, on occasion, made people reconsider their tactical assumptions mid-fight."}</p>
<p className="chg">{"You gain +3 to Chakra Exhaustion rolls. "}<del>{"When"}</del><ins>{"Whenever"}</ins>{" you "}<del>{"calculate the"}</del><ins>{"acquire a"}</ins>{" Chakra Exhaustion penalty from "}<ins>{"using"}</ins>{" a jutsu, "}<del>{"treat the jutsu's Chakra cost as 4 lower (minimum 5)."}</del><ins>{"that penalty is reduced by 1."}</ins>{" When you Rest, your Chakra Exhaustion penalty drops by one more than your Stamina penalty does. The Upkeep of all your active jutsu is reduced by 1."}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"_Upgrades From: Expansive Chakra_ <br />_Upgrades Into: Regenerative Chakra or Surging Power_"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"<span id=\"bruiser\"></span>**Bruiser**"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"The phrase \"hits harder than they look\" was coined for someone like you. It isn't about size. It's about knowing exactly how to put everything you have into a single point of contact."}</p>
<p className="chg">{"Your Physical Damage Bonus increases by RES/30. You gain DR equal to STR/5. All damage-dealing CQC attacks gain Stun 2, or have their existing Stun increased by 2."}<del>{"CQC Grapple techniques do not benefit this Stun"}</del></p>
<p className="ctx">{"_Upgrades From: Martial Prowess_ <br />_Upgrades Into: Relentless_<br />"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"<span id=\"cunning\"></span>**Cunning**"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"Other people's certainty is an open door to you. You understand the architecture of belief, the particular way people expect things to go, and how to build an illusion inside that expectation. There is something fundamentally unsettling about how easily you do this."}</p>
</div>
<div className="hunk">
<p className="lineno">{"Line 45"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"The combined Chakra Control and Research requirement for learning Genjutsu does not apply to you. You gain +2 to all Genjutsu rolls. A target doesn't gain their repeat-use defensive bonus until the third time you use the same Genjutsu against them in a battle rather than the second. You may reduce the Upkeep of one Genjutsu at a time by CHA/25, to a floor of 0, and may switch which technique receives this benefit any time you activate a Genjutsu with an Upkeep."}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"<span id=\"elementalist\"></span>**Elementalist**"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"What most shinobi manage in one element, you manage in two. and with a refinement that draws sidelong glances from people who've been working at it for years longer than you have."}</p>
<p className="chg">{"You may use two elements of your choice as though you had Elemental Aptitude for them, without consuming Aptitude ranks. Status effects from the five base elements in your Elemental Ninjutsu have their severity increased by "}<del>{"1"}</del><ins>{"1, which is doubled if they're applied with a status roll."}</ins></p>
<p className="ctx">{"_Upgrades From: Elementally Gifted_ <br />_Upgrades Into: Elemental Mastery_"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"<span id=\"endless-energy\"></span>**Endless Energy**"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"You do not get tired. This is genuinely baffling to watch. Your body processes exertion differently, and the margin before it starts affecting your performance is several multiples of what it is for ordinary people. You've never quite understood the feel."}</p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"When you calculate the"}</del><ins>{"Whenever your"}</ins>{" Stamina penalty "}<del>{"from a technique, treat the technique's Stamina cost as 2 lower (minimum 5)."}</del><ins>{"increases, it increases by 1 less."}</ins>{" The first time you fail a Stamina roll in any battle, you advance one fewer Fatigue category. You gain a bonus to Stamina rolls equal to RES/40. Every 15 IC, your Stamina penalty decreases by 1 on its own."}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"_Upgrades From: Indefatigable_<br />_Upgrades Into: Surging Power_<br /><br />"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"<span id=\"exceptionally-fast\"></span>**Exceptionally Fast**"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"You do not simply move quickly. You have internalized speed to the degree that your actions look wrong to people watching, like frames are missing between where you were and where you are."}</p>
</div>
<div className="hunk">
<p className="lineno">{"Line 77"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"Your Ranged Weapon attacks gain DEX/20 additional Range and +1% damage per yard between you and your target, to a maximum of 15 + DEX/10%. When you hit with a Ranged Weapon attack, you are treated as having hit by 1 more than you did for purposes of Partial Success and hit-margin modifiers. During a Multi-Throw, you may throw DEX/40 additional weapons without contributing to the action’s Speed or cost."}</p>
<p className="chg">{"You may perform a special Parry "}<ins>{"using one or more Small Thrown weapons"}</ins>{" by combining it with a Basic Weapon Attack or Multi-Throw as an Interrupt. Add Weapon Parry’s Speed to the attack as a link. The combined action’s final Speed must be equal to or less than the incoming attack’s final Speed (not half, as usual). If the Weapon Parry succeeds, the attacker must defend against your attack using your Weapon Parry roll as its Weapon Accuracy. You must use at least as many weapons as the attacker is, all of the same type. "}<del>{"Accuracy bonuses"}</del><ins>{"Abilities"}</ins>{" that "}<ins>{"modify the attack’s cost or"}</ins>{" reduce "}<del>{"costs"}</del><ins>{"the attack’s damage"}</ins>{" do not apply "}<ins>{"their accuracy bonus"}</ins>{" to "}<del>{"the"}</del><ins>{"your"}</ins>{" Parry roll."}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"_Upgrades From: Quick Draw_ <br />_Upgrades Into: Relentless_<br />"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"<span id=\"insidious\"></span>**Insidious**"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"Your plans have plans, and your contingencies have contingencies, and at no point in the process have you told anyone what any of them are. The smallest incidental damage is part of a sequence that only becomes legible at the very end."}</p>
</div>
</div>

<p className="chg">{"You have peerless control over elemental chakra, far beyond the realm of what's considered normal, and to a level that some would even think is impossible. Choose two elements. <p className="ctx">{"Additionally, you have an Elemental Shaping pool which dictates how much you can modify any given basic Elemental Ninjutsu (advanced elements can not be shaped), with one point in it for each element you have Aptitude for, and one point for each rank of each basic element you know a jutsu of (for a potential maximum of 30). You can use your Elemental Shaping pool to modify any Elemental Ninjutsu you perform in the following ways (which doesn’t ‘use up’ your pool; its size simply determines how much you can modify any given technique):"}</p>

<p className="chg">{"- "}<del>{"10 points: Change the damage type (Blunt, Energy, Piercing, or Slashing) to another damage type."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- 8"}</del><ins>{"12"}</ins>{" points: +1 "}<del>{"Damage Bonus"}</del><ins>{"Ninjutsu Accuracy"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg">{"- "}<del>{"6"}</del><ins>{"10"}</ins>{" points: Add an effect from "}<del>{"another element not its own:"}</del><ins>{"your most recent elemental jutsu’s type so long as it differs from your currently used element, based on your current jutsu’s Rank (R):"}</ins>{" Stun "}<del>{"1"}</del><ins>{"R \\* 2"}</ins>{" (Doton), Burns "}<del>{"1"}</del><ins>{"R \\* 2"}</ins>{" (Katon), "}<del>{"Paralysis 1"}</del><ins>{"Shock R"}</ins>{" for "}<del>{"10"}</del><ins>{"20"}</ins>{" IC (Raiton), "}<del>{"-1"}</del><ins>{"-R"}</ins>{" Chakra Cost (Suiton), Knockback "}<del>{"1 yard"}</del><ins>{"R \\* 2 yards"}</ins>{" (Fuuton)"}</p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"- 8 points: +1 Ninjutsu Damage Bonus"}</ins></p>

<p className="ctx">{"- 2 points: +1 Range"}</p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"- 1 point: +1 Damage"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"There are some exceptions to the above:"}</del>{"For "}<del>{"jutsu which launch projectiles at targets (but not are not AoE, such as Houenka), additional targets gives a single extra projectile. Similarly, any technique which does not list Area or Range cannot have them added to it to gain those qualities. The status effects added from Shaping (aside from Suiton’s, which is automatic) do not require a status roll, but do require you to hit with a damage-dealing ninjutsu attack, and accordingly do not apply to environmental effects (such as Hidden Mist, or Yomi Numa) though they can be added to techniques which already have them, so long as they’re a valid choice. <p className="chg"><ins>{"There are some exceptions to the above: jutsu which are Environmental, and/or do not have a “ - Damage: “ stat block can not have flat damage added to them. <p className="chg"><ins>{"The status effects added from Shaping (aside from Suiton’s) do not require a Status roll, but do require you to hit with a damage-dealing ninjutsu attack, and accordingly do not apply to Environmental jutsu, though they can be added to jutsu which already have them, so long as they’re a valid choice. <p className="chg">{"You can pick any of the options multiple "}<del>{"times,"}</del><ins>{"times except for the added status effect,"}</ins>{" limited only by the size of your Shaping pool. "}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"_Upgrades From: Elementalist_"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"<span id=\"regenerative-chakra\"></span>**Regenerative Chakra**"}</p>

The second is a Major Energy, the third a Severe Blunt, and the fourth a Critical Blunt."}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"For example, if you wounded yourself using the gates six times, and rolled head three times, left arm once, right arm once, and chest once, you would have: a minor energy head wound, a major energy head wound, a severe blunt head wound, two minor energy arm wounds, and a minor energy chest wound, for a total of six different wounds. Additionally, any wound-healing abilities (such as some Medical ninjutsu, Henzaki Organ Mobility, or Kaguya Realignment) cannot be used while the gates are still open: you have to close them first."}</p>

<p className="chg"><del>{"- You always know the exact distance and direction to your sword, regardless of barriers or chakra interference. This sense cannot be blocked or detected."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- **Recall** (Speed 4, 3 AP). As an action, you may recall your sword to your hand from anywhere within CHA yards. <p className="chg"><del>{"- **Flying Blade** (Speed 10, Chakra 5, Stamina 8). You command your sword to attack independently at range. <p className="chg"><del>{"- You cannot be disarmed of this sword by any means. Attempts to forcibly remove it from your grasp automatically fail. The sword remains in your hand unless you willingly release it."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- The sword resists all sealing techniques. Any attempt to seal it into a scroll, storage container, or fuuinjutsu effect automatically fails. <p className="chg"><del>{"- If separated from you (e.g., you are knocked unconscious and the sword is taken), any creature other than you who attempts to wield it suffers a -6 penalty to Accuracy and cannot activate any of its magical properties. <p className="chg"><del>{"- If the sword is broken or destroyed, the bond is severed and this technique must be reapplied during a reforging ritual (requiring four weeks and 1500 Ryo of materials). <div className="hunk">

<p className="ctx">{"Effects: This adds your physical and ninjutsu damage bonuses together to determine its damage bonus. The primary target takes full damage; anyone else caught in the area takes only the damage from your damage bonuses."}</p>
<p className="chg">{"**-=[ Sanshoku no Gan'yaku - Three-Colored Pills "}<del>{"]=**"}</del><ins>{"]=-**"}</ins></p>
<p className="ctx">{"Costs: 1 Talent"}</p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"<br />Effects: [Read here](https://nw-shotai.mintlify.site/Akimichi_talent)"}</del><ins>{"A secret medicine developed and used exclusively by the Akimichi clan, these three pills convert stored energy into chakra to give the user a tremendous boost in power. Understandably, these are exceptionally potent in the hands of an Akimichi."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"Even so, there's a good reason they're not widely used. They're not gentle on the body, and the chemicals released by the pills in the process are actually toxic."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"Eating a pill is a Speed 4 action. Despite their names, they don't taste like food. They taste like... well, poison."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"All pills have some effects in common. When you use a pill, you will gain some amount of Calorie Points. This is based on your true maximum (10 per rank of Calorie Conversion), and can take you above it. While under the effects of pills, you suffer no penalties from having too many CP. Just like with Baika and Cho Baika, increases to attributes does not increase any of your Accuracies."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"When a pill's effects end, you lose CP equal to however many it gave you."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"Anyone other than an Akimichi who takes these pills suffers half the base Poison for half the base duration, and receives none of the benefits."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"When a pill's effects expire, you can let it end or take a higher-strength pill as a non-action. So long as you are under the effects of one of the three pills, you ignore all penalties caused by these pills."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"If you use one while another is in effect (for example, you take the Green pill, and 20 IC later you take the Yellow pill), you overwrite all the effects, including the Poison, of the lower-strength pill, but keep any extra calories you have from it."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"Akimichi are issued only one of each pill at a time. Giving these pills away is treason, and it's a credit to the clan that to date no Akimichi has done so."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"---"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"–( **Hourengan – Green Spinach Pill** )–"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"You gain half your maximum Calorie Points. The ratios for spending CP improve, becoming 1:2 for Akimichi clan jutsu and 3:1 for non-clan jutsu."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"For the duration, you are immune to knockback and have all your attributes increased by 5%. When you take the pill, your Chakra Exhaustion penalty is reset to 0."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"When this ends, your Fatigue is increased by 2 categories."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"- Duration: 80 + RES/5"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"- Poison: 3 - (RES/50)"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"---"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"–( **Karengan – Yellow Curry Pill** )–"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"You gain your maximum Calorie Points. The ratios for spending CP improve, becoming 1:3 for Akimichi clan jutsu and 2:1 for non-clan jutsu."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"For the duration, you are immune to knockback, reduce all Stun you take by RES/30 (stacking with any other sources of Stun reduction), and have all your attributes increased by 15%."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"When you take the pill, your Chakra Exhaustion and Stamina penalties are reset to 0."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"When this ends, your Fatigue is increased by 4 categories."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"- Duration: 60 + RES/5"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"- Poison: 6 - (RES/50)"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"---"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"–( **Tongarashigan – Red Chilli Pill** )–"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"You gain double your maximum Calorie Points. The ratios for spending CP improve, becoming 1:5 for Akimichi clan jutsu and 1:1 for non-clan jutsu."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"For the duration, you are immune to knockback and Stun, and the effects of Paralysis are divided by four (round down). All your attributes are increased by 25%."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"When you take the pill, your Chakra Exhaustion and Stamina penalties are reset to 0 and your Fatigue is lowered by 2 categories."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"When this ends, your Fatigue is increased by 8 categories."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"- Duration: 40 + RES/5"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"- Poison: 9 - (RES/40)"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"---"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"As you may notice, it's quite easy to go above \"Fatigue 6,\" which is to say, unconsciousness, by using these pills. Pushing your body that far is dangerous, and hard to recover from."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"For every Fatigue category you are beyond unconscious (i.e. Fatigue 8 would be 2 over), one Fatigue level takes an entire OOC day to heal."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"For example, if you were at Fatigue 9, you would be (7, 8, 9) 3 levels above unconscious. This means that once you woke up, it would take an OOC day to recover from Fatigue 5, another OOC day to recover from Fatigue 4, another OOC day to recover from Fatigue 3, and you'd then recover normally."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"After this effect takes place, you cannot regain any Calorie Points or receive the benefits of food pills (they're still poison, though) until you've fully healed from all Fatigue."}</ins></p>
<p className="ctx">{"<br /><br />"}</p>
</div>

<p className="chg">{"## "}<del>{"**[Minor]"}</del><ins>{"**[Major]"}</ins>{" Inuzuka**"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"The Inuzuka continue to be the Southern Province's most economical asset, and their renowned devotion is used as a promise of compliance. They are the Internal Customs Agents and Wardens of the Fraying Edge, the human barrier that upholds the Land of Fire's manufactured wealth, and they are no longer merely scouts. Because their Ninken were developed for an era of economic warfare, they can track not just enemy shinobi but also illegal goods, data seals that are prohibited, and the chemical odor of unregistered medicine reserves that desperate citizens are hoarding. The Root uses them to police the traffic flowing between Konoha proper and the impoverished puppet states (Rain, Sound), ensuring the realm remains orderly. As blunt, tireless Instruments, they are constantly stationed in the suffocating grime of the border posts, where their Taijutsu is a non-lethal tool for \"asset retrieval\", breaking limbs to subdue debtors, smugglers, and anyone who threatens this fragile lie."}</p>

<p className="ctx">{"Your ninken, or ninja dog, is more than just an extra weapon in combat; it's a living creature with its own capabilities. However, its advancement is inextricably tied to your own as the two of you do all your training together."}</p>
<p className="chg">{"Your Ninken has the same stats that you have in everything besides AP, WP, "}<ins>{"movement,"}</ins>{" PDB, Vitality and HP. "}<ins>{"It is always a bit faster than you, moving at 125% of your base movement."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg">{"- Basic Attack: Speed 8, "}<del>{"(STR/6+2)d6"}</del><ins>{"(STR/6)d6 or (AGI/6)d6, your choice"}</ins></p>
<p className="chg">{"- Grab: Same as "}<del>{"an E-rank Grapple."}</del><ins>{"the E-Rank CQC technique Grab."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"- Grapples: Your Ninken can perform any Grapple-Opener or other grapple technique you know."}</ins></p>
<p className="ctx">{"- Stealth: Your Ninken hides! This is the same speed as a normal hide action, and has all the same requirements."}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"- Ninken are assumed to pass all stamina checks they have to make, but will advance in fatigue when you do (They’re empathetic like that), they don’t track a vitality pool, but if they’re hit by an attack they calculate what kind of wound they would take based on their Max Vitality (Which as a reminder, is 50% of your vitality)."}</p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"- Your Ninken can Dodge, Parry and Block, but can't Take the Hit. When you Take the Hit for your Ninken, roll 2d20 and take the higher."}</ins></p>
<p className="ctx">{"They don’t take wounds, but they receive a stun based on the rank of the wound they would have taken."}</p>

<p className="lineno">{"Line 349"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"**Juugo Suto have +5% Bonus XP.**"}</p>
<p className="chg">{"-=[ **Survival Instinct** ]=-<br />Most clans pass on secret techniques as a rite of passage. The Juugo Suto have a somewhat different rite: once a member is ready they are left alone, in the middle of the desert, without provisions or equipment, and told not to return for at least a month; most of them aren’t even teenagers. It’s a testament to the clan that virtually every student is, in fact, ready to undertake this challenge, and returns safely to the village. It’s a testament to the ordeal that they inevitably return from their "}<del>{"journey:"}</del><ins>{"journey changed:"}</ins>{" quieter, calmer, and infinitely more aware of the world around them."}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"- Type: Ability"}</p>
</div>
<div className="hunk">
<p className="lineno">{"Line 358"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"You are immune to surprise attacks. Attacks which would normally not end an opponent’s stealth still do not do so, but they receive none of the benefits of surprise attacks against you. Sneak attacks against you instead follow the normal rules for surprise attacks."}</p>
<p className="chg">{"-=[ **Tenacity** ]=-<br "}<del>{"/>The desert"}</del><ins>{"/>Desert"}</ins>{" is some of the most difficult terrain to navigate, but the sand foxes do it. They adapt to the harsh environment. It’s not that it gets any easier, or that they’ve learned any secret. They just face the obstacles, assess them, accept them, and do what they must regardless."}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"- Type: Ability"}</p>

<p className="ctx">{"You base penalties from Visibility and Immobility status effects as though they are one severity lower than they actually are, per Rank. This does not change the actual severity of the status you are affected by (for things such as Hiding if you are suffering from a -6 or higher Visibility status effect)."}</p>

<p className="ctx">{"-=[ **Desert Ghosts** ]=-<br />In the world of shinobi, there is no such thing as hunter and hunted; one is both, at all times. The Juugo Suto are preeminently aware of this. That awareness, and their mentality of never taking their safety for granted, has led to no end of frustration for foreign shinobi trying to gauge the strength and frequency of Suna’s patrols."}</p>
</div>
<div className="hunk">

<p className="chg"><del>{"## **[Major] Tsuchiya Kuchi**"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"Nobody remembers who first brought the scroll west. What is known is this: somewhere in Iwagakure's deep archive sat a kinjutsu so old the Stone had forgotten why they banned it. It allowed the caster to knead chakra directly into physical matter,  clay, stone, sand, anything pliable,  and infuse it with explosive release from the inside out. The technique required the subject to cast it on themselves first. The result was mouths. Wet, living, chakra-conducting mouths carved into the palms of the hands."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"The first person to use it found the art they desired the moment their clay detonated and took down their pursuers. Every person since has reported the same thing. The explosion doesn't feel like destruction, rather pure ecstasy.  The scroll surfaced in the Western Province during the early chaos of the civil war. Nobody claims responsibility for moving it. The scroll passes hand to hand. It finds people at their lowest and offers them something permanent and irreversible in exchange for everything they thought they knew about themselves. The mouths cannot be hidden without bandages. They cannot be removed. Every person who undergoes the process comes out the other side changed."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"**--( Rumor Has It )--**"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- **Iwa Nin:** You can spot them by their hands if they're not careful about bandaging. Most aren't. Most don't care. The Regency offered three of them a salary and a compound and they blew the negotiator's chair into the ceiling."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- **Other Nin:** Don't try to take them alive. Don't try to negotiate. They fight like every battle is the last one they'll ever want to fight, and they make it look like something worth watching."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"**–=[ Clay Creation ]=–**<br />The foundation of all Tsuchiya techniques. Members of this clan chew chakra-infused clay and shape it into living constructs using their hands and mouth, breathing explosive chakra directly into the material."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- **Type**: Ability"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- **Base** Cost: 5"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- **Scale**: 0"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- **Limit**: CHA/10"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"Effects:"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"You gain a Clay Pool of 5 per rank. Clay Pool represents the amount of usable clay you have prepared at any given time. You recover CHA/5 Clay Pool per day, or half that if you cannot access your clay supply between scenes."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"Clay creatures are divided into three sizes: Small, Medium and Large."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- Small clay construct costs 1 Clay Pool to create."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- Medium clay construct costs 3 Clay Pool to create."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- Large clay costs construct 5 Clay Pool to create."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"Clay creatures may use your CHA in all relevant stat calculations and your NDB when dealing damage or STR and PDB, depending on which is higher and inherit any unique you have that enhances taijutsu. They do not have their own Vitality, instead they have HP equal to CHA per size category and the speed needed in creating them is attached."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- Small: CHA HP, speed 6,"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- Medium CHA\\*2 HP,  speed 8,"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"Medium: CHA × 2 HP, speed 8. When created, you may designate a Medium construct to resemble yourself or an ally. While active, you are considered Hidden Among Clones (as per Bunshin no Jutsu), with you and the construct counting as two clones for miss chance and Observe actions. You are aware of its location, and it of yours. When destroyed, it bursts into sticky clay, anyone within 5 yards must make a Dodge roll against the construct's Accuracy (using your NInjutsu accuracy +3). On failure, they gain Sticky 2. This Sticky does not count toward the normal Sticky cap and persists until removed."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- Large: CHA\\*3 HP, speed 12"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"By spending 15 Chakra in battle as a speed 10 action, you can create a supplementary 10 Clay pool.  When a clay creature is destroyed before detonating naturally, it explodes immediately as an uncontrolled detonation, dealing half its HP as damage in a 5 yard radius to everyone in that area. However it cannot wound and for targets with DR, the DR is considered halved. Raiton ninjutsu can be used to damage the clay puppets without its explosion being triggered."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"**–=[ Rapid Molding ]=–**<br />The hand-mouths learn faster than the mind ever does. Where others knead each construct with care, you have grown comfortable with the rhythm of creation itself, the pulse of clay, the flex of chakra, the instinctive knowledge of when a shape is complete before it's even finished. Rapid Molding is not a technique. It is the difference between thinking about the motion and simply letting it happen."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- Type: Ability"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- Base Cost: 30"}</del></p>

<p className="chg"><del>{"Effects: Tsuchiya with this ability have grown adept at forming multiple smaller constructs at once, enabling them to change the speed of creation to X+Y/2, where X is the base speed and Y is the number of constructs being made. <p className="chg"><del>{"<br />**–( Sticky )–**"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"This is a Status Effect representing clay adhered to the target's body. Sticky reduces movement speed by 15% per stack, this does not however stack with immobilization, this effect would always supersede it when checking for movement speed penalties in any relevant interaction."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"Sticky stacks fully with itself from the same source. Multiple Sticky statuses from different clay pieces all stack together into a single total penalty with a cap of CHA/20 of the user who applied the sticky first, though the movement reduction can never reduce a target below 10% of their base movement speed."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"For every point of Sticky attached to the body, it increases the damage dice of explosion techniques by +2d20. Bakuton is the element released from the Sticky, it deals blunt damage. As a speed 10 action, you may detonate the Sticky dealing the 2d20 damage per sticky only. The target may not defend against this. Bakuton is considered as a General Ninjutsu."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"Sticky does not fade over time on its own. It ends when the clay causing it is destroyed or detonated. Removing a piece of small or medium clay  is a Speed 10 action. Removing a large clay requires escaping the grapple through normal grapple escape rules, where the Tsuchiya uses either their PDB or NDB +3."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"**–( Chiisana tobibi — Flying Minuscule Explosions)–**"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"Small living clay constructs thrown or spat from the palm-mouths. They skitter across terrain, camouflage against skin and cloth, and wait patiently for the command."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- **Type**: Bakuton Ninjutsu, D-rank"}</del></p>

<p className="chg"><del>{"**Detonation on Impact:** Choose this mode when throwing. Each clay that hits deals Blunt damage. <p className="chg"><del>{"**Crawl:** Clay that misses the target in an attack may be declared to Crawl by spending an extra chakra cost of 5 and speed 5 as an interrupt. <p className="chg"><del>{"**–( Tsuchi Ningyou — Clay Doll )–**<br />A larger construct, shaped quickly between the palms and hurled. It doesn't explode on contact. Most people spend their last few seconds trying to pull it off and wondering why it feels warm."}</del></p>

<p className="chg"><del>{"You launch the construct at the target. This is treated as a Taijutsu Grapple attack for the purposes of how it is defended against and escaped. Your grapple check modifier uses the higher of NDB or PDB + CHA/10 or STR/10 as the attacker. On a hit, the target is Grappled by the construct and gains Sticky +2 if it is Medium or +3 if it is Large. Both values may exceed the normal Sticky cap as long as the grapple is actively maintained. While declaring the attack you may use the effect of a D-rank CQC Solo Taijutsu attack, paying its cost as X."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"When you use this technique, declare a countdown value from 1 to CHA/20. This value is Y. The construct detonates after 5+Y IC have passed. For every point of Y, the detonation's damage increases by 2%. This bonus is additive with Sticky damage. Each failed Break Grapple or Shift Grapple attempt by the target reduces your countdown by 1. If countdown reaches 0 by this means, the construct detonates immediately at its current damage. If you drop the Upkeep, the construct deactivates and releases the grapple but does not detonate. You may control other clay constructs normally while this Upkeep is maintained."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"**–( Hanabi no Hinkon — Poor Man's Rose)–**<br />The Tsuchiya Kuchi do not speak of its origin.  A poor man's rose is a flower of beauty and the flora of necessity, something that blooms only in ash and smoke, where there is nothing left to burn but yourself. The ecstasy of the combustion, the realization that destruction could feel so right. Every use since has been a prayer to that moment, a repetition of the act that first set the clan free. Art becomes an EXPLOSION!"}</del></p>

Anyone within the explosion's area gains Suffocation equal to half the primary target's Sticky status, rounded down and degraded by 1 every 5 ic."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"The explosion itself leaves a lingering chemical residue. Anyone who takes damage from this technique, primary target or splash, is exposed to a Poison of Severity X+2 with a duration of 80 IC. This poison does not require a status roll,  it applies automatically to anyone who takes damage from it."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"The explosion has an area of (Sticky \\* 2) yards centered on the target. Anyone within this area other than the primary target takes half the total damage. This splash damage cannot wound but inherits the .open() threshold of the primary detonation, and still applies Suffocation and Poison as described above. After detonation all Sticky status on the target is removed. All clay used in this detonation is consumed and cannot be recovered. Clay Dolls detonated this way do not use their current damage percentage increase from their countdown at the moment this technique is activated."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"If the target has 0 Sticky status, this technique still functions but deals only (CHA/10)[d20.open](http://d20.open)(16) damage with no area effect, no Suffocation, and Poison 4 only if damage is dealt."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"You may detonate clay on a target beyond your range by paying an additional 25 Chakra, but the open threshold is fixed at 18 regardless of Sticky status, the Suffocation effect does not apply, and the Poison severity is reduced to 1."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"**-=[ C12 ]=-** <br />You have cultivated your clay beyond the basics. The hand-mouths that mark your palms have grown hungrier, and the clay you shape has begun to remember the shape of chakra itself. Some say the mouths whisper to you now, offering secrets of creation that only the truly devoted can hear. The line between what you control and what controls you has become... negotiable."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- Type: Ability"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- Cost: 1 Talent Point"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- Limit: 1 <br />Effects: You gain a second Clay Pool, separate from your primary reserve, with a capacity equal to your CHA/10. This secondary pool recovers at half the normal rate (CHA/10 per day) and can only be replenished through combat, by absorbing residual chakra from explosions or reclaiming clay from removed constructs. You convert chakra-infused clay back into usable energy. For every 5 Clay Pool points you sacrifice from your primary or secondary reserve, you reduce the Chakra cost of your next Bakuton technique by 2 (to a minimum of half its original cost). This reduction is applied before other cost modifiers. When creating or deploying any Bakuton construct (including Chiisana Tobibi, Tsuchi Ningyou, or other clay techniques), you may spend additional Clay Pool points from either reserve to improve the construct's performance: (You may only apply this effect twice)"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"Effects:"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- 5 CP +2 Damage Dice"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- 8 CP +2 Accuracy"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- 10 CP Construct gains +60 HP OR applies +1 Sticky on hit"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"These bonuses are declared at the time of creation. You may only spend up to your CHA/10 Clay Pool points on a single construct in this way."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"You detonate your secondary Clay Pool in a controlled blast that vents volatile chakra without consuming your life. This is a Speed 16 Interrupt action that may be declared even when it is not your turn. The explosion erupts from your position with an Area equal to CHA/10 yards (minimum 5), dealing (Secondary Clay Pool × 3)[d20.open](http://d20.open)(8) Blunt damage to everyone within range, including yourself. Damage Reduction is halved against this explosion, and it cannot be blocked. Targets may Dodge using your Nin accuracy + 3 as Accuracy; success halves damage and avoids Sticky. All targets caught in the blast gain Sticky 2; you may designate any number of allies who take half damage and receive no Sticky. Your Fatigue advances by 3 categories immediately, if this reaches Fatigue 5 or higher, you fall unconscious at the end of the action. You take damage equal to your maximum Vitality (DR applies, applied after the explosion's damage to others). Your entire secondary Clay Pool is consumed and reduced to zero. You cannot use any clay techniques or Bakuton jutsu for 50 IC as your body and hand-mouths recover. The shockwave violently expels any foreign substances or restraints, you may immediately remove one immobilizing effect or grapple condition of your choice as part of this action."}</del></p>
<p className="ctx">{"## **[Minor] Sabechi**"}</p>

<p className="ctx">{"The technique involves a specialized chakra-conversion in the lungs that halts just before ignition. <div className="hunk">
<p className="lineno">{"Line 40"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"By transferring your chakra to the recipient, you can stimulate the body to begin the recovery process and help stabilize their most basic functions—breath rate, pulse, the little things that keep a person from dying while you figure out the bigger problems. It lacks the precision of more advanced jutsu to target specific injuries, but helps to keep people alive until they can receive advanced care. The technique was originally developed for battlefield triage, where the choice was often \"keep them alive now or lose them forever.\" It's not elegant. It's not efficient. It's the difference between a closed casket and an open one."}</p>

<p className="ctx">{"**Effects:** Allows a single kunai or senbon to be used as a compass for 6 hours or until used in an attack. It's not quite perfect, but it's good enough for most uses."}</p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"Alternatively, the user can attach wire to themselves (typically a wrist or ankle, but any direct contact can do) and to a Kunai (and only Kunai, for this purpose), and then use this technique to charge the Kunai and either throw it, or stick it in the ground at their feet. Once placed, it ‘charges’ an Area 10 space around itself with a faint hum of buzzing chakra. It is not enough to be visible or noticeable to most (though most non-aggressive animals will instinctively avoid the area, and thus not trigger it), though it is easily detected by Chakra Sensors and Doujutsu capable of seeing chakra who view it (and those who know the technique can immediately recognize it for what it is)."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"Any person leaving, or entering, the area charged once it has been set causes the Raiton chakra to feedback to anyone who is attached to the Kunai through wire, immediately waking them up and alerting them to the fact that someone tripped this technique’s effect, as well as ending this technique. Likewise, someone severing the wire connection (such as through a Kunai being thrown from outside the area) also causes it to trigger."}</ins></p>
<p className="chg"><ins>{"Usage of this technique in combat will alert the user whether someone enters and leaves the area it’s placed at, but will not reveal someone’s location who is in stealth, or even pinpoint the direction in which they crossed the barrier: It is too simplistic for that. Once placed, the effect can last up to eight hours."}</ins></p>

<Accordion title={"Rules"} description={"/Jutsu/rules · +10 −831 words"}>
<div className="rev-diff">
<div className="hunk">
<p className="lineno">{"Line 196"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"## Combos"}</p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"Combos are a special type of unarmed taijutsu attack which benefit from being used in rapid succession. There are three types of taijutsu attacks related to combos: Openers, Combos, and Finishers."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"### Power Attack and Unarmed Focus"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"When a combo jutsu references your Basic Unarmed Attack, it includes any ranks of Unarmed Focus you might have, and can benefit from any ranks of Power Attack you have and wish to apply. Any time a jutsu says 'd\\*', this is an improvement to the base damage, and likewise benefits from Power Attack. Power Attack does not apply to any dice that have a size listed (d6, d8, d12, etc), only d\\*."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"### Openers"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"Openers are not always, themselves, Combo moves, though some are. Openers can be used without being in a Combo, and after an opener hits, you may launch into a combo (i.e. use a Combo jutsu). Openers are marked as such in their entry, usually by \"Special: Opener.\""}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"Using an Opener when you are already in a Combo has no effect: You can not get the bonuses of multiple Openers for a Combo and can not change which Opener effect you have for your Combo after you've started a Combo. If the Opener itself is not a Combo jutsu, however (such as with Kage Buyou), using it will still break your Combo."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"### Combos"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"Combo moves indicate as much in their name (typically being named \"\\_\\_\\_ Combo\"), or by being in the Taijutsu Combo category. You cannot use a combo jutsu unless the last non-Interrupt action you took was a Combo or Opener, or the Combo move itself has an Upkeep; similarly, if your combo is broken (see below), you have to start with a new opener. When you begin a combo, you have a \"Combo Counter\"– basically, the number of combo moves you've strung together. This starts at 1 when you hit with an opener. Each time you hit with a combo, the combo counter increases by 1."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"Each combo will have a combo counter entry; for example, here is Lotus Combo's:"}</del>{"Combo "}<del>{"Counter: +2d10 damage, +2 Stamina, +1 Accuracy"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"This works as might be expected: You multiply those bonuses by the number of combo counters you've amassed. Thus, if you opened with a Kage Buyou and proceeded to a Lotus Combo,"}</del><ins>{"rules (technique tags,"}</ins>{" the"}<del>{"first hit would have +1 Accuracy, +2d10 damage, and +2 to its Stamina cost (because you're at"}</del>{" Combo "}<del>{"1 after the opener); if it hit you'd progress up to Combo 2, and the next combo you used would have +2 Accuracy, +4d10 damage, and +4 Stamina cost."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"Some combos or finishers have notation like +3d\\*. These are based on your basic unarmed attack damage to some degree, and the \\* in these cases will always refer to the die size of your basic unarmed attacks, based on how much Power Attack you're using on that combo component, so with all four ranks of Power Attack, for instance, it would be adding d12s."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"### Switching Combos"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"Once you've begun a combo, any other combo jutsu is fair game. You could freely alternate between Lion and Lotus combos as you pleased. For the most part, things remain simple. Each move increases your Combo Counter normally, no matter how many times you switch between combo moves. Your Combo Counter bonuses are based only on the move you're currently using. For example, say you used Kage Buyou as an opener, then 4 Lotus Combos, followed by 3 Lion Combos. This would give you a total Combo of 8, assuming they all hit (1 + 4 + 3). If your next Combo move was a Lion combo, you'd multiply its CC bonuses (+1 Accuracy and -1 Speed) by 8, giving you a total of +8 Accuracy and -8 Speed (though this would put it at only Speed 5, because it still can't be reduced below half its base Speed)."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"### Finishers"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"The final type of move is finishers. Finishers may be used on their own, but have a special effect if used at the end of a combo. Though they do not receive the stacking effects for consecutive combo hits, they have special effects based on your total combo counters. For example, the Lion Barrage finisher has the following entry: Combo Bonus: +2d10 damage For each combo counter you have when you execute Lion Barrage, its damage is increased by 2d10."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"###"}</del><ins>{"Counter,"}</ins>{" Combo Breaking"}<del>{"When a combo is broken, your combo counter is immediately set to 0, you may not continue using combo moves,"}</del>{" and "}<del>{"all accrued combo counter bonuses"}</del><ins>{"grapples)"}</ins>{" are "}<del>{"lost. Combo breaking occurs if any of"}</del><ins>{"in"}</ins>{" the "}<del>{"following things happen:"}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- You use any non-combo jutsu (even if it's an interrupt)."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- You take a non-combo, non-interrupt action."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- You execute a finisher."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- You attack a new target."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"- You are Stunned."}</del></p>
<p className="chg"><del>{"Hitting a clone"}</del><ins>{"Core Rules"}</ins>{" of "}<del>{"your intended target does not count as attacking a new target, and accordingly does not break your combo, however hitting a clone (of any target) does not increase your combo counter."}</del><ins>{"[CQC Taijutsu](/Jutsu/cqc-taijutsu#core-rules)."}</ins></p>
<p className="ctx">{"---"}</p>
</div>
</div>
</Accordion>

<div className="rev-diff">

<p className="lineno">{"Line 5"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"Considered the modern successor to cavalry mounts, motor vehicles have become the standard for rapid deployment and heavy support in the Confederacy's military operations. Motorcycles, jeeps, armored cars, trucks, boats, and even light helicopters allow shinobi to cover vast distances, carry heavy equipment, and bring overwhelming firepower to bear. The drawbacks are equally obvious: engines are loud, vehicles cannot sneak, fuel is a constant concern, and a well‑placed Raiton jutsu can turn a truck into a smoking wreck. Still, for those willing to trade stealth for speed and armor, a vehicle is an invaluable asset."}</p>
<p className="chg">{"Vehicles are purchased with Savings, though members of Major clans may use Requisition at half the listed price."}<del>{"You must have at least Rank 1 of the Leadfoot ability before purchasing or operating any vehicle in combat or hazardous conditions. <p className="chg">{"A small but highly polished and lacquered calabash gourd with a cap at the top and a bright scarlet rope to secure it to one's belt, the monkey's gourd doesn't look like it should be able to hold more than half a gallon of liquid. Regardless, the gourd can be summoned once "}<del>{"a week (OOC day)."}</del><ins>{"per OOC day."}</ins></p>
<p className="ctx">{"- **Water** — The small gourd actually contains 20 gallons of water that can be used in jutsu, or for any normal purpose. The gourd lasts for 1 hour and can be refilled, but anything other than water put into it will curiously spill out despite the holder's best efforts."}</p>

<p className="ctx">{"**Effects:** Each summoner with this contract is assigned their own unique scroll toad, and unless a special deal is made with the toads only ever has that one scroll toad to call upon. The scroll toad sticks around to do its job (having things written on it), and then leaves or climbs into the summoner's stomach (inflicting a Stun 40 on the summoner). Retrieving a scroll stored this way is a Speed 20 action."}</p>
<p className="chg">{"Nobody but the summoner themselves can retrieve the scroll from their stomach; if the summoner is killed, the toad will simply unsummon itself. Normally, information on the scroll will be stored indefinitely, but the toad can, at the summoner's request, wipe part or all of the scroll clean. Once the summoner has decided he no longer needs the scroll, the toad can climb out and will head home. After being unsummoned, the Scroll Toad cannot be called upon again for eight "}<del>{"weeks."}</del><ins>{"OOC days."}</ins></p>
<p className="ctx">{"---"}</p>

<p className="ctx">{"While being capable of speech, diving toads will frequently complain that people \"all look the same\" to it. Otherwise, the toad won't be able to recognize them."}</p>
<p className="chg">{"The diving toad can only be summoned once every four "}<del>{"weeks."}</del><ins>{"OOC days."}</ins></p>

<p className="lineno">{"Line 232"}</p>
<p className="ctx">{"That said, they expect to receive an appropriate amount of respect for their efforts, and be treated as a valued underling, not a disposable thug. Leaving them in combat while you flee (which is not the same as having them cover your escape, then unsummoning them once you've got a head start) constitutes a Breach of Contract."}</p>
<p className="chg">{"Additionally, if the Guardian is unsummoned due to injuries (vitality dropping to zero) it cannot be summoned again for four "}<del>{"weeks."}</del><ins>{"OOC days."}</ins></p>
